[30/04/26, 2:22:50 AM] Udit Jain : #include <DHT.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>

// ============================================================================
// PIN CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================================

// Sensor Pins (Analog)
const int ACS712_PIN = A0;          // ACS712 current sensor output
const int VOLTAGE_SENSOR_PIN = A1;  // 0-25V voltage divider
const int LIGHT_SENSOR_PIN = A2;    // KY-018 photoresistor (analog)

// Sensor Pins (Digital)
const int PIR_MOTION_PIN = 2;       // HC-SR501 motion sensor
const int DHT_PIN = 3;              // DHT11 data pin

// Relay Control Pins (4-Channel Relay Module)
const int RELAY_1_PIN = 4;          // Relay 1 - Thermal Sensor
const int RELAY_2_PIN = 5;          // Relay 2 - Motion Sensor
const int RELAY_3_PIN = 6;          // Relay 3 - Light Sensor
const int RELAY_4_PIN = 7;          // Relay 4 - Humidity Sensor

// ============================================================================
// SENSOR CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================================

#define DHTTYPE DHT11
DHT dht(DHT_PIN, DHTTYPE);

// ACS712-5A specifications: 185 mV per Amp
const float ACS712_SENSITIVITY = 0.185; // Volts per Amp
const float ADC_REFERENCE = 5.0;        // Arduino reference voltage
const int ADC_RESOLUTION = 1024;        // 10-bit ADC

// Voltage sensor: voltage divider (10k + 2.2k resistors for 0-25V)
const float VOLTAGE_DIVIDER_RATIO = (10.0 + 2.2) / 2.2; // R1+R2 / R2

// ============================================================================
// MISSION MODES & STATE VARIABLES
// ============================================================================

enum MissionMode {
  PATROL = 0,
  SURVEILLANCE = 1,
  STEALTH = 2,
  ALERT = 3
};

struct SystemState {
  MissionMode currentMode;
  MissionMode recommendedMode;
  unsigned long lastUpdateTime;
  uint8_t powerReadiness;
  uint8_t threatIndex;
  int resilience_window_minutes;
  uint8_t visibilityIndex;
  uint8_t inferenceConfidence;
  bool anomalyDetected;
};

struct SensorReadings {
  float temperature;
  float humidity;
  float lightLevel;
  bool motionDetected;
  float batteryVoltage;
  float currentDraw;
  float powerConsumption;
};

struct RelayState {
  bool relay1;  // Thermal
  bool relay2;  // Motion
  bool relay3;  // Light
  bool relay4;  // Humidity
};

// Global variables
SystemState system_state = {PATROL, PATROL, 0, 75, 15, 480, 30, 85, false};
SensorReadings sensors = {0, 0, 0, false, 12.0, 0.5, 6.0};
RelayState relays = {true, true, true, true};

// Smoothing variables
const int SMOOTHING_SAMPLES = 10;
float voltageReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
float currentReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
float tempReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
int smoothing_index = 0;  // FIX: will now be incremented after each read cycle

// Anomaly detection
unsigned long last_anomaly_time = 0;
const unsigned long ANOMALY_PERSISTENCE_MS = 5000;
float baseline_temp = 20.0;
float baseline_humidity = 50.0;

// FIX: Baseline flag — so first real DHT read sets the baseline
bool baseline_set = false;

// DHT timing — DHT11 needs at least 2 seconds between reads
unsigned long last_dht_read = 0;
const unsigned long DHT_READ_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;

// ============================================================================
// INITIALIZATION
// ============================================================================

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(9600);
  delay(1000);

  pinMode(PIR_MOTION_PIN, INPUT);

  pinMode(RELAY_1_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_2_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_3_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_4_PIN, OUTPUT);

  // FIX: LOW activates relay (active-low), so all ON at boot = LOW
  digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, LOW);

  dht.begin();

  // Initialize smoothing arrays with safe defaults
  for (int i = 0; i < SMOOTHING_SAMPLES; i++) {
    voltageReadings[i] = 12.0;
    currentReadings[i] = 0.5;
    tempReadings[i] = 20.0;
  }

  Serial.println("{\"status\":\"VANGUARD AI initialized\"}");
}

// ============================================================================
// MAIN LOOP
// ============================================================================

void loop() {
  readAllSensors();
  updateSystemState();
  updateRelayControl();
  sendTelemetryPacket();
  processIncomingCommands();
  delay(500);
}

// ============================================================================
// SENSOR READING FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

void readAllSensors() {
  readVoltage();
  readCurrent();
  readDHTSensor();
  readLightSensor();
  readMotionSensor();

  // FIX: Increment and wrap smoothing index AFTER all sensors read
  smoothing_index = (smoothing_index + 1) % SMOOTHING_SAMPLES;
}

void readVoltage() {
  int raw = analogRead(VOLTAGE_SENSOR_PIN);
  float voltage = (raw / (float)ADC_RESOLUTION) * ADC_REFERENCE * VOLTAGE_DIVIDER_RATIO;

  voltageReadings[smoothing_index] = voltage;
  sensors.batteryVoltage = getAverage(voltageReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);

  system_state.powerReadiness = constrain(
    map(sensors.batteryVoltage * 100, 900, 1500, 0, 100),
    0,
    100
  );
}

void readCurrent() {
  int raw = analogRead(ACS712_PIN);
  float voltage = (raw / (float)ADC_RESOLUTION) * ADC_REFERENCE;
  float midpoint = ADC_REFERENCE / 2.0;
  float current = (voltage - midpoint) / ACS712_SENSITIVITY;

  currentReadings[smoothing_index] = abs(current);
  sensors.currentDraw = getAverage(currentReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);
  sensors.powerConsumption = sensors.batteryVoltage * sensors.currentDraw;
}

void readDHTSensor() {
  // FIX: Enforce 2-second minimum interval for DHT11
  unsigned long now = millis();
  if (now - last_dht_read < DHT_READ_INTERVAL_MS) {
    return; // Not enough time passed, skip this read
  }
  last_dht_read = now;

  float humidity = dht.readHumidity();
  float temperature = dht.readTemperature();

  if (isnan(humidity) || isnan(temperature)) {
    return; // Skip on bad read
  }

  tempReadings[smoothing_index] = temperature;
  sensors.temperature = getAverage(tempReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);
  sensors.humidity = humidity;

  // FIX: Use a flag instead of checking baseline_temp == 0
  if (!baseline_set) {
    baseline_temp = temperature;
    baseline_humidity = humidity;
    baseline_set = true;
  }
}

void readLightSensor() {
  int raw = analogRead(LIGHT_SENSOR_PIN);
  sensors.lightLevel = map(raw, 0, 1023, 0, 100);
  system_state.visibilityIndex = sensors.lightLevel;
}

void readMotionSensor() {
  sensors.motionDetected = digitalRead(PIR_MOTION_PIN) == HIGH;
}

// ============================================================================
// INFERENCE & DECISION LOGIC
// ============================================================================

void updateSystemState() {
  calculateThreatIndex();
  detectAnomalies();
  recommendMissionMode();
  calculateResilienceWindow();

  system_state.inferenceConfidence = constrain(
    (system_state.powerReadiness + 100 - system_state.threatIndex) / 2,
    0,
    100
  );

  system_state.lastUpdateTime = millis();
}

void calculateThreatIndex() {
  int threat = 0;

  if (sensors.motionDetected) {
    threat += 40;
  }

  float temp_deviation = abs(sensors.temperature - baseline_temp);
  if (temp_deviation > 5.0) {
    threat += 20;
  } else if (temp_deviation > 2.0) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  float humidity_deviation = abs(sensors.humidity - baseline_humidity);
  if (humidity_deviation > 20.0) {
    threat += 15;
  }

  if (sensors.lightLevel < 20) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  // FIX: Use powerConsumption (V*I) for consistency, not raw currentDraw
  if (sensors.powerConsumption > 10.0) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  system_state.threatIndex = constrain(threat, 0, 100);
}

void detectAnomalies() {
  bool current_anomaly = false;

  if (sensors.motionDetected) current_anomaly = true;
  if (abs(sensors.temperature - baseline_temp) > 5.0) current_anomaly = true;
  if (abs(sensors.humidity - baseline_humidity) > 25.0) current_anomaly = true;

  if (current_anomaly) {
    last_anomaly_time = millis();
    system_state.anomalyDetected = true;
  } else if (millis() - last_anomaly_time > ANOMALY_PERSISTENCE_MS) {
    system_state.anomalyDetected = false;
  }
}

void recommendMissionMode() {
  MissionMode recommended = PATROL;

  if (system_state.powerReadiness < 20) {
    recommended = STEALTH;
  } else if (system_state.threatIndex > 60) {
    recommended = ALERT;
  } else if (system_state.threatIndex > 30) {
    recommended = SURVEILLANCE;
  } else if (system_state.powerReadiness < 40) {
    recommended = STEALTH;
  } else {
    recommended = PATROL;
  }

  system_state.recommendedMode = recommended;
}

void calculateResilienceWindow() {
  if (sensors.powerConsumption > 0.1) {
    float available_wh = sensors.batteryVoltage * 5.0; // Assume 5Ah battery
    float hours_remaining = available_wh / sensors.powerConsumption;
    system_state.resilience_window_minutes = constrain((int)(hours_remaining * 60), 0, 999);
  } else {
    system_state.resilience_window_minutes = 999;
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// RELAY CONTROL & DUTY CYCLING
// ============================================================================

void updateRelayControl() {
  boolean relay1_state = true;
  boolean relay2_state = true;
  boolean relay3_state = true;
  boolean relay4_state = true;

  switch (system_state.currentMode) {
    case PATROL:
    case SURVEILLANCE:
    case ALERT:
      relay1_state = true;
      relay2_state = true;
      relay3_state = true;
      relay4_state = true;
      break;

    case STEALTH:
      relay1_state = true;   // Keep thermal
      relay2_state = true;   // Keep motion
      relay3_state = false;  // Disable light (save power)
      relay4_state = false;  // Disable humidity (save power)
      break;
  }

  // Active-low: LOW = relay ON, HIGH = relay OFF
  digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, relay1_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, relay2_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, relay3_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, relay4_state ? LOW : HIGH);

  relays.relay1 = relay1_state;
  relays.relay2 = relay2_state;
  relays.relay3 = relay3_state;
  relays.relay4 = relay4_state;
}

// ============================================================================
// TELEMETRY & COMMUNICATION
// ============================================================================

void sendTelemetryPacket() {
  StaticJsonDocument<512> doc;

  doc["timestamp"] = millis();
  doc["packet_age"] = 0;

  JsonObject sensors_obj = doc.createNestedObject("sensors");
  sensors_obj["temperature"] = round(sensors.temperature * 10) / 10.0;
  sensors_obj["humidity"] = round(sensors.humidity);
  sensors_obj["light"] = sensors.lightLevel;
  sensors_obj["motion"] = sensors.motionDetected;
  sensors_obj["voltage"] = round(sensors.batteryVoltage * 100) / 100.0;
  sensors_obj["current"] = round(sensors.currentDraw * 100) / 100.0;
  sensors_obj["power"] = round(sensors.powerConsumption * 100) / 100.0;

  JsonObject threat_obj = doc.createNestedObject("threat_stack");
  threat_obj["thermal"] = sensors.temperature;
  threat_obj["humidity"] = sensors.humidity;
  threat_obj["light"] = sensors.lightLevel;
  threat_obj["motion"] = sensors.motionDetected ? 100 : 0;
  // FIX: Use powerConsumption normalized, consistent with threat calculation
  threat_obj["load"] = constrain((int)(sensors.powerConsumption * 10), 0, 100);
  threat_obj["threat_index"] = system_state.threatIndex;

  JsonObject state_obj = doc.createNestedObject("system_state");
  state_obj["current_mode"] = getModeString(system_state.currentMode);
  state_obj["recommended_mode"] = getModeString(system_state.recommendedMode);
  state_obj["power_readiness"] = system_state.powerReadiness;
  state_obj["threat_index"] = system_state.threatIndex;
  state_obj["resilience_window"] = system_state.resilience_window_minutes;
  state_obj["visibility_index"] = system_state.visibilityIndex;
  state_obj["inference_confidence"] = system_state.inferenceConfidence;
  state_obj["anomaly_detected"] = system_state.anomalyDetected;

  JsonObject relay_obj = doc.createNestedObject("relay_control");
  relay_obj["relay_1_thermal"] = relays.relay1;
  relay_obj["relay_2_motion"] = relays.relay2;
  relay_obj["relay_3_light"] = relays.relay3;
  relay_obj["relay_4_humidity"] = relays.relay4;

  serializeJson(doc, Serial);
  Serial.println();
}

String getModeString(MissionMode mode) {
  switch (mode) {
    case PATROL:      return "PATROL";
    case SURVEILLANCE: return "SURVEILLANCE";
    case STEALTH:     return "STEALTH";
    case ALERT:       return "ALERT";
    default:          return "UNKNOWN";
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// COMMAND PROCESSING
// ============================================================================

void processIncomingCommands() {
  if (Serial.available()) {
    String command = Serial.readStringUntil('\n');
    command.trim();

    StaticJsonDocument<256> cmd_doc;
    DeserializationError error = deserializeJson(cmd_doc, command);

    if (error) {
      Serial.print("{\"error\":\"Parse failed: ");
      Serial.print(error.c_str());
      Serial.println("\"}");
      return;
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("set_mode")) {
      String mode_str = cmd_doc["set_mode"];
      if (mode_str == "PATROL")           system_state.currentMode = PATROL;
      else if (mode_str == "SURVEILLANCE") system_state.currentMode = SURVEILLANCE;
      else if (mode_str == "STEALTH")     system_state.currentMode = STEALTH;
      else if (mode_str == "ALERT")       system_state.currentMode = ALERT;
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Mode changed\"}");
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("set_baseline")) {
      baseline_temp = cmd_doc["baseline_temp"] | 20.0;
      baseline_humidity = cmd_doc["baseline_humidity"] | 50.0;
      baseline_set = true;
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Baseline reset\"}");
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("relay_override")) {
      int relay_num = cmd_doc["relay_num"];
      boolean state = cmd_doc["state"];

      switch (relay_num) {
        case 1: digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay1 = state; break;
        case 2: digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay2 = state; break;
        case 3: digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay3 = state; break;
        case 4: digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay4 = state; break;
      }
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Relay updated\"}");
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

float getAverage(float* array, int size) {
  float sum = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    sum += array[i];
  }
  return sum / size;
}

/*
 * FIXES APPLIED:
 * 1. Relay init changed to LOW (active-low = ON at boot)
 * 2. baseline_set flag added — first real DHT read sets baseline correctly
 * 3. smoothing_index now increments after every read cycle
 * 4. DHT11 2-second minimum interval enforced via last_dht_read timer
 * 5. threat_stack "load" now uses powerConsumption (consistent with threat calc)
 *
 * NOTE: ESP32/LCD integration not included here.
 * Send telemetry to ESP32 via SoftwareSerial or I2C separately.
 *
 * SERIAL COMMAND FORMAT (Dashboard → Arduino):
 * {"set_mode": "STEALTH"}
 * {"relay_override": {"relay_num": 3, "state": false}}
 * {"set_baseline": {"baseline_temp": 20.5, "baseline_humidity": 48.0}}
 */

[22/05/26, 1:50:46 PM] Udit Jain : Create a professional, modern, and high-impact pitch deck presentation for an Ideathon. The presentation is for a product called "Sentinel Prime" (Repository Health Intelligence). 

Use a sleek, engineering-first, and professional tone. Structure the presentation into exactly 8 slides with the following detailed content and layout:

---
Slide 1: Title & Hook
- Title: Sentinel Prime
- Subtitle: Unified Engineering Intelligence & Repository Health Platform
- Visual Cue: A dark-themed, sleek abstract network graph representing codebases.
- Key Message: Moving from reactive firefighting to proactive, AI-driven codebase governance.

Slide 2: The Problem (The Hidden Cost of Tech Debt & Silos)
- Headline: The Codebase Blindspot
- Key Pain Points:
  1. Silent Architectural Decay: Circular imports and dependency sprawl slow down release cycles.
  2. The "Bus Factor" Risk: Critical knowledge silos where only 1-2 developers understand core systems.
  3. High-Churn Hotspots: Bug-prone files with high complexity and frequent changes that break constantly.
  4. Context Overhead: Technical managers lack real-time, actionable insights into repo health.

Slide 3: The Solution (Introducing Sentinel Prime)
- Headline: A Single Pane of Glass for Codebase Health
- Core Value: Sentinel Prime mines git commit history and parses dependency imports to compute real-time, actionable codebase health metrics.
- Key Pillars:
  - Repository Health Score (0-100 index based on complexity, churn, and architecture).
  - Codebase Hotspot Analysis.
  - Developer Collaboration Graph.
  - AI-Driven Architectural Copilot.

Slide 4: Core Features & Capabilities
- Headline: Features Built for Modern Engineering Teams
- Bullet Points:
  - Code & Architecture Health Index: Custom algorithms calculating complexity deltas and file churn.
  - Interactive Hotspots: Visualizing files with high complexity and frequent updates to prevent production regressions.
  - Developer Collaboration Graph: Network visualization mapping authors to files to detect knowledge silos.
  - Sentinel Copilot: A context-driven RAG AI assistant (using Vector DB and LLMs like DeepSeek & Groq) providing architectural explanations of repository health changes.

Slide 5: Technical Architecture
- Headline: Under the Hood of Sentinel Prime
- Architecture Flow:
  1. Git Mining Engine: Extracts and parses local/remote git commits and file modifications.
  2. AST Analyzer: Maps import/export dependencies to identify architectural circular loops.
  3. LLM API & Vector DB: Runs RAG queries on codebase metadata using high-performance open-source models (Groq/DeepSeek).
  4. Modern Web App Frontend: A glassmorphic, responsive, reactive dashboard built with React/Vite.

Slide 6: Target Market & User Personas
- Headline: Who Needs Sentinel Prime?
- Personas:
  - Engineering Managers & VPs: To track team health, bus factors, and maintain development velocity.
  - Tech Leads & Architects: To spot architectural drift, high-risk commits, and circular dependencies early.
  - Startup Founders: To keep codebase quality high during rapid scaling.

Slide 7: Business Model & ROI
- Headline: Maximizing Developer Velocity & ROI
- Value Proposition:
  - Reduced onboarding time by visualizing codebase structure.
  - Preventing expensive production outages by spotting "Most Dangerous Commits".
  - Proactive technical debt management instead of expensive rewrites.
- Delivery Model: SaaS-based developer tool (integration with GitHub Actions, GitLab CI/CD, and standalone web dashboards).

Slide 8: The Vision & Future Roadmap
- Headline: The Future of Autonomous Code Governance
- Timeline:
  - Phase 1: Real-time Repository Diagnostics & Sentinel Copilot (Current).
  - Phase 2: IDE Integrations (VS Code / JetBrains) warning developers of high-churn risks pre-commit.
  - Phase 3: Auto-refactoring agents that proactively fix architectural coupling.
- Closing Statement: "Sentinel Prime: Giving engineers the map to navigate their own code."
---

Format the output as a fully detailed, slide-by-slide text presentation script containing slide titles, subtitles, slide layout recommendations (such as split columns, icons, and diagrams), and presenter notes.

[20/06/26, 9:19:04 PM] Udit Jain : Forecasting and/or Nowcasting of Solar Flares using combined Soft and Hard X-ray data from Aditya-L1
Description
Solar flares are sudden, intense bursts of radiation originating from the release of magnetic energy in the solar atmosphere. These events can trigger severe space weather disruptions, impacting Earth-bound satellite communications, GPS navigation, and power grids. Anticipating these disruptions is critical for infrastructure safeguarding.
ISRO's Aditya-L1 mission continuously monitors the Sun from Lagrange Point L1 using two X-ray payloads: SoLEXS (Solar Low Energy X-ray Spectrometer, capturing soft X-rays) and HEL1OS (High Energy L1 Orbiting X-ray Spectrometer, capturing hard X-rays).
This challenge requires participants to build an automated algorithmic pipeline that uses the combined time-series data from SoLEXS and HEL1OS to detect (nowcast) or predict (forecast) these solar flares.
Objectives
To design an automated solar flare detection algorithm for the nowcasting (real-time detection and classification) of solar flares using soft and hard X-ray data.
To build a predictive algorithm for the forecasting of solar flares by identifying precursor patterns in soft and hard X-ray light curves before actual solar flare occurs.
Expected Outcomes
An automated database of nowcasted solar flares, generated by combining soft and hard X-ray light curves.
A trained model capable of forecasting flares with a quantifiable lead time.
Interface that visualizes the X-ray light curves and triggers with visual alerts when a flare is nowcasted or forecasted.
Dataset Required
Primary Data: Aditya-L1 SoLEXS (Level-1) and HEL1OS (Level-1) data, accessible via the ISRO ISSDC PRADAN portal.
Participants can use any other supplementary datasets.
Suggested Tools/Technologies
SoLEXS and HEL1OS data processing software accessible via the ISRO ISSDC PRADAN portal.
Any other open-source tools.
Expected Solution / Steps to be followed to achieve the objectives
Download SoLEXS and HEL1OS from the ISSDC portal. Write scripts to read SoLEXS and HEL1OS time-series light curve data.
Visually understand typical solar flare light curve observed in soft and hard X-rays.
Algorithmic Nowcasting: Build algorithms to detect flares independently in both soft and hard X-rays. Combine these independent catalogues to generate a master catalogue.
Predictive Modelling: Train a time-series model using combined dataset to predict the probability of a flare occurring in the next N minutes.
Evaluation Criteria
Detection of low- and high-class flares
High True Positive Rate and low False Alarm Rate for predictions.
Lead Time of predictions: How many minutes before the flare peak the model triggers an alert.

[11/12/25, 12:14:10 PM] Rudra: """
Make sure to install openpyxl before running this program:
pip install openpyxl
"""

from openpyxl import load_workbook

# Load the Excel file
workbook = load_workbook("studData.xlsx")
sheet = workbook.active

# Initialize variables to track topper
topperUsn = ""
topperName = ""
topperCgpa = -1  # Start with very low CGPA

# Loop through the rows (assuming header in first row)
for row in sheet.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True):
    usn, name, cgpa = row  # Assuming columns: USN, Name, CGPA
    if cgpa > topperCgpa:
        topperCgpa = cgpa
        topperName = name
        topperUsn = usn

# Display first topper
print("--- First Topper of the Class ---")
print(f"USN: {topperUsn}")
print(f"Name: {topperName}")
print(f"CGPA: {topperCgpa}")
[11/12/25, 12:22:57 PM] Rudra: """
Make sure to install openpyxl before running this program:
pip install openpyxl
"""

import sys
from openpyxl import Workbook

# Check if user provided N as command-line argument
if len(sys.argv) != 2:
    print("Usage: python multiplicationTable.py N")
    sys.exit(1)

# Read N from command-line
n = int(sys.argv[1])

# Create a new workbook and select the active sheet
workbook = Workbook()
sheet = workbook.active
sheet.title = f"{n}x{n} Multiplication Table"

# Fill multiplication table
for i in range(1, n + 1):
    for j in range(1, n + 1):
        sheet.cell(row=i, column=j, value=i * j)

# Save workbook to Excel file
fileName = f"MultiplicationTable_{n}x{n}.xlsx"
workbook.save(fileName)

print(f"Multiplication table saved to {fileName}")

[12/04/26, 1:54:29 PM] Udit Jain : #include <DHT.h>

#define DHTPIN 2
#define DHTTYPE DHT11

DHT dht(DHTPIN, DHTTYPE);

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(9600);
  dht.begin();
}

void loop() {
  float temp = dht.readTemperature();
  float hum = dht.readHumidity();

  if (isnan(temp) || isnan(hum)) {
    Serial.println("Sensor not working");
  } else {
    Serial.print("Temp: ");
    Serial.print(temp);
    Serial.print(" °C  |  Humidity: ");
    Serial.println(hum);
  }

  delay(2000);
}

[25/04/26, 12:28:17 AM] Udit Jain : Project Description — VANGUARD AI

VANGUARD AI is an autonomous, edge-intelligent power management system designed for mission-critical defence environments where reliability, efficiency, and rapid decision-making are essential.

The system uses a distributed hardware architecture consisting of an Arduino Uno for real-time environmental sensing and an ESP32 for control and communication. Multiple sensors—including light, temperature, current, voltage, and motion—continuously monitor operational conditions.

This data is processed locally using an embedded AI decision engine, enabling the system to:

* Detect anomalies and faults in real time
* Prioritize mission-critical loads
* Optimize power consumption dynamically
* Operate fully offline without cloud dependency

Based on intelligent analysis, the system controls electrical loads through relay modules, ensuring that essential systems remain active even under constrained resources.

VANGUARD AI is designed for deployment in forward operating bases, command bunkers, field hospitals, and remote defence installations, where it enhances operational safety, reduces energy waste, and improves mission continuity.

[29/04/26, 7:35:55 PM] Udit Jain : Core ML Model Work
	•	Train/refine your Decision Tree model with synthetic data covering all sensor combinations (light, temp, humidity, current, voltage, motion)
	•	Build a data simulator that generates realistic sensor readings for PIR, ACS712, and voltage — so your model trains on complete feature sets
	•	Add model evaluation: accuracy scores, confusion matrix, feature importance visualization
Dashboard Upgrades
	•	Extend your existing dashboard to show placeholder panels for current, voltage, and motion — ready to wire in tomorrow
	•	Add the 3 operational mode switcher (Normal / Resource Constraint / Stealth) with mode-based UI changes
	•	Build a fault detection alert panel that triggers on anomaly thresholds
AI Logic Modules
	•	Load prioritization engine: given simulated current + voltage, decide which relay channels to cut in Resource Constraint mode
	•	Anomaly detection: flag abnormal current/voltage spikes using your Decision Tree or a simple threshold classifier
	•	Energy optimization scoring: compute a “waste score” from temp + light + simulated load data
MQTT Integration Prep
	•	Set up your MQTT broker and define topic schema for all sensors (/vanguard/pir, /vanguard/current, etc.)
	•	Build subscriber handlers in Flask so the backend is ready to receive real sensor data the moment hardware connects
Stealth Mode Logic
	•	Implement the EM signature reduction algorithm — basically a relay scheduling policy that staggers switching to minimize detectable power fluctuations

[30/04/26, 2:22:50 AM] Udit Jain : #include <DHT.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>

// ============================================================================
// PIN CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================================

// Sensor Pins (Analog)
const int ACS712_PIN = A0;          // ACS712 current sensor output
const int VOLTAGE_SENSOR_PIN = A1;  // 0-25V voltage divider
const int LIGHT_SENSOR_PIN = A2;    // KY-018 photoresistor (analog)

// Sensor Pins (Digital)
const int PIR_MOTION_PIN = 2;       // HC-SR501 motion sensor
const int DHT_PIN = 3;              // DHT11 data pin

// Relay Control Pins (4-Channel Relay Module)
const int RELAY_1_PIN = 4;          // Relay 1 - Thermal Sensor
const int RELAY_2_PIN = 5;          // Relay 2 - Motion Sensor
const int RELAY_3_PIN = 6;          // Relay 3 - Light Sensor
const int RELAY_4_PIN = 7;          // Relay 4 - Humidity Sensor

// ============================================================================
// SENSOR CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================================

#define DHTTYPE DHT11
DHT dht(DHT_PIN, DHTTYPE);

// ACS712-5A specifications: 185 mV per Amp
const float ACS712_SENSITIVITY = 0.185; // Volts per Amp
const float ADC_REFERENCE = 5.0;        // Arduino reference voltage
const int ADC_RESOLUTION = 1024;        // 10-bit ADC

// Voltage sensor: voltage divider (10k + 2.2k resistors for 0-25V)
const float VOLTAGE_DIVIDER_RATIO = (10.0 + 2.2) / 2.2; // R1+R2 / R2

// ============================================================================
// MISSION MODES & STATE VARIABLES
// ============================================================================

enum MissionMode {
  PATROL = 0,
  SURVEILLANCE = 1,
  STEALTH = 2,
  ALERT = 3
};

struct SystemState {
  MissionMode currentMode;
  MissionMode recommendedMode;
  unsigned long lastUpdateTime;
  uint8_t powerReadiness;
  uint8_t threatIndex;
  int resilience_window_minutes;
  uint8_t visibilityIndex;
  uint8_t inferenceConfidence;
  bool anomalyDetected;
};

struct SensorReadings {
  float temperature;
  float humidity;
  float lightLevel;
  bool motionDetected;
  float batteryVoltage;
  float currentDraw;
  float powerConsumption;
};

struct RelayState {
  bool relay1;  // Thermal
  bool relay2;  // Motion
  bool relay3;  // Light
  bool relay4;  // Humidity
};

// Global variables
SystemState system_state = {PATROL, PATROL, 0, 75, 15, 480, 30, 85, false};
SensorReadings sensors = {0, 0, 0, false, 12.0, 0.5, 6.0};
RelayState relays = {true, true, true, true};

// Smoothing variables
const int SMOOTHING_SAMPLES = 10;
float voltageReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
float currentReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
float tempReadings[SMOOTHING_SAMPLES];
int smoothing_index = 0;  // FIX: will now be incremented after each read cycle

// Anomaly detection
unsigned long last_anomaly_time = 0;
const unsigned long ANOMALY_PERSISTENCE_MS = 5000;
float baseline_temp = 20.0;
float baseline_humidity = 50.0;

// FIX: Baseline flag — so first real DHT read sets the baseline
bool baseline_set = false;

// DHT timing — DHT11 needs at least 2 seconds between reads
unsigned long last_dht_read = 0;
const unsigned long DHT_READ_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;

// ============================================================================
// INITIALIZATION
// ============================================================================

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(9600);
  delay(1000);

  pinMode(PIR_MOTION_PIN, INPUT);

  pinMode(RELAY_1_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_2_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_3_PIN, OUTPUT);
  pinMode(RELAY_4_PIN, OUTPUT);

  // FIX: LOW activates relay (active-low), so all ON at boot = LOW
  digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, LOW);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, LOW);

  dht.begin();

  // Initialize smoothing arrays with safe defaults
  for (int i = 0; i < SMOOTHING_SAMPLES; i++) {
    voltageReadings[i] = 12.0;
    currentReadings[i] = 0.5;
    tempReadings[i] = 20.0;
  }

  Serial.println("{\"status\":\"VANGUARD AI initialized\"}");
}

// ============================================================================
// MAIN LOOP
// ============================================================================

void loop() {
  readAllSensors();
  updateSystemState();
  updateRelayControl();
  sendTelemetryPacket();
  processIncomingCommands();
  delay(500);
}

// ============================================================================
// SENSOR READING FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

void readAllSensors() {
  readVoltage();
  readCurrent();
  readDHTSensor();
  readLightSensor();
  readMotionSensor();

  // FIX: Increment and wrap smoothing index AFTER all sensors read
  smoothing_index = (smoothing_index + 1) % SMOOTHING_SAMPLES;
}

void readVoltage() {
  int raw = analogRead(VOLTAGE_SENSOR_PIN);
  float voltage = (raw / (float)ADC_RESOLUTION) * ADC_REFERENCE * VOLTAGE_DIVIDER_RATIO;

  voltageReadings[smoothing_index] = voltage;
  sensors.batteryVoltage = getAverage(voltageReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);

  system_state.powerReadiness = constrain(
    map(sensors.batteryVoltage * 100, 900, 1500, 0, 100),
    0,
    100
  );
}

void readCurrent() {
  int raw = analogRead(ACS712_PIN);
  float voltage = (raw / (float)ADC_RESOLUTION) * ADC_REFERENCE;
  float midpoint = ADC_REFERENCE / 2.0;
  float current = (voltage - midpoint) / ACS712_SENSITIVITY;

  currentReadings[smoothing_index] = abs(current);
  sensors.currentDraw = getAverage(currentReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);
  sensors.powerConsumption = sensors.batteryVoltage * sensors.currentDraw;
}

void readDHTSensor() {
  // FIX: Enforce 2-second minimum interval for DHT11
  unsigned long now = millis();
  if (now - last_dht_read < DHT_READ_INTERVAL_MS) {
    return; // Not enough time passed, skip this read
  }
  last_dht_read = now;

  float humidity = dht.readHumidity();
  float temperature = dht.readTemperature();

  if (isnan(humidity) || isnan(temperature)) {
    return; // Skip on bad read
  }

  tempReadings[smoothing_index] = temperature;
  sensors.temperature = getAverage(tempReadings, SMOOTHING_SAMPLES);
  sensors.humidity = humidity;

  // FIX: Use a flag instead of checking baseline_temp == 0
  if (!baseline_set) {
    baseline_temp = temperature;
    baseline_humidity = humidity;
    baseline_set = true;
  }
}

void readLightSensor() {
  int raw = analogRead(LIGHT_SENSOR_PIN);
  sensors.lightLevel = map(raw, 0, 1023, 0, 100);
  system_state.visibilityIndex = sensors.lightLevel;
}

void readMotionSensor() {
  sensors.motionDetected = digitalRead(PIR_MOTION_PIN) == HIGH;
}

// ============================================================================
// INFERENCE & DECISION LOGIC
// ============================================================================

void updateSystemState() {
  calculateThreatIndex();
  detectAnomalies();
  recommendMissionMode();
  calculateResilienceWindow();

  system_state.inferenceConfidence = constrain(
    (system_state.powerReadiness + 100 - system_state.threatIndex) / 2,
    0,
    100
  );

  system_state.lastUpdateTime = millis();
}

void calculateThreatIndex() {
  int threat = 0;

  if (sensors.motionDetected) {
    threat += 40;
  }

  float temp_deviation = abs(sensors.temperature - baseline_temp);
  if (temp_deviation > 5.0) {
    threat += 20;
  } else if (temp_deviation > 2.0) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  float humidity_deviation = abs(sensors.humidity - baseline_humidity);
  if (humidity_deviation > 20.0) {
    threat += 15;
  }

  if (sensors.lightLevel < 20) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  // FIX: Use powerConsumption (V*I) for consistency, not raw currentDraw
  if (sensors.powerConsumption > 10.0) {
    threat += 10;
  }

  system_state.threatIndex = constrain(threat, 0, 100);
}

void detectAnomalies() {
  bool current_anomaly = false;

  if (sensors.motionDetected) current_anomaly = true;
  if (abs(sensors.temperature - baseline_temp) > 5.0) current_anomaly = true;
  if (abs(sensors.humidity - baseline_humidity) > 25.0) current_anomaly = true;

  if (current_anomaly) {
    last_anomaly_time = millis();
    system_state.anomalyDetected = true;
  } else if (millis() - last_anomaly_time > ANOMALY_PERSISTENCE_MS) {
    system_state.anomalyDetected = false;
  }
}

void recommendMissionMode() {
  MissionMode recommended = PATROL;

  if (system_state.powerReadiness < 20) {
    recommended = STEALTH;
  } else if (system_state.threatIndex > 60) {
    recommended = ALERT;
  } else if (system_state.threatIndex > 30) {
    recommended = SURVEILLANCE;
  } else if (system_state.powerReadiness < 40) {
    recommended = STEALTH;
  } else {
    recommended = PATROL;
  }

  system_state.recommendedMode = recommended;
}

void calculateResilienceWindow() {
  if (sensors.powerConsumption > 0.1) {
    float available_wh = sensors.batteryVoltage * 5.0; // Assume 5Ah battery
    float hours_remaining = available_wh / sensors.powerConsumption;
    system_state.resilience_window_minutes = constrain((int)(hours_remaining * 60), 0, 999);
  } else {
    system_state.resilience_window_minutes = 999;
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// RELAY CONTROL & DUTY CYCLING
// ============================================================================

void updateRelayControl() {
  boolean relay1_state = true;
  boolean relay2_state = true;
  boolean relay3_state = true;
  boolean relay4_state = true;

  switch (system_state.currentMode) {
    case PATROL:
    case SURVEILLANCE:
    case ALERT:
      relay1_state = true;
      relay2_state = true;
      relay3_state = true;
      relay4_state = true;
      break;

    case STEALTH:
      relay1_state = true;   // Keep thermal
      relay2_state = true;   // Keep motion
      relay3_state = false;  // Disable light (save power)
      relay4_state = false;  // Disable humidity (save power)
      break;
  }

  // Active-low: LOW = relay ON, HIGH = relay OFF
  digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, relay1_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, relay2_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, relay3_state ? LOW : HIGH);
  digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, relay4_state ? LOW : HIGH);

  relays.relay1 = relay1_state;
  relays.relay2 = relay2_state;
  relays.relay3 = relay3_state;
  relays.relay4 = relay4_state;
}

// ============================================================================
// TELEMETRY & COMMUNICATION
// ============================================================================

void sendTelemetryPacket() {
  StaticJsonDocument<512> doc;

  doc["timestamp"] = millis();
  doc["packet_age"] = 0;

  JsonObject sensors_obj = doc.createNestedObject("sensors");
  sensors_obj["temperature"] = round(sensors.temperature * 10) / 10.0;
  sensors_obj["humidity"] = round(sensors.humidity);
  sensors_obj["light"] = sensors.lightLevel;
  sensors_obj["motion"] = sensors.motionDetected;
  sensors_obj["voltage"] = round(sensors.batteryVoltage * 100) / 100.0;
  sensors_obj["current"] = round(sensors.currentDraw * 100) / 100.0;
  sensors_obj["power"] = round(sensors.powerConsumption * 100) / 100.0;

  JsonObject threat_obj = doc.createNestedObject("threat_stack");
  threat_obj["thermal"] = sensors.temperature;
  threat_obj["humidity"] = sensors.humidity;
  threat_obj["light"] = sensors.lightLevel;
  threat_obj["motion"] = sensors.motionDetected ? 100 : 0;
  // FIX: Use powerConsumption normalized, consistent with threat calculation
  threat_obj["load"] = constrain((int)(sensors.powerConsumption * 10), 0, 100);
  threat_obj["threat_index"] = system_state.threatIndex;

  JsonObject state_obj = doc.createNestedObject("system_state");
  state_obj["current_mode"] = getModeString(system_state.currentMode);
  state_obj["recommended_mode"] = getModeString(system_state.recommendedMode);
  state_obj["power_readiness"] = system_state.powerReadiness;
  state_obj["threat_index"] = system_state.threatIndex;
  state_obj["resilience_window"] = system_state.resilience_window_minutes;
  state_obj["visibility_index"] = system_state.visibilityIndex;
  state_obj["inference_confidence"] = system_state.inferenceConfidence;
  state_obj["anomaly_detected"] = system_state.anomalyDetected;

  JsonObject relay_obj = doc.createNestedObject("relay_control");
  relay_obj["relay_1_thermal"] = relays.relay1;
  relay_obj["relay_2_motion"] = relays.relay2;
  relay_obj["relay_3_light"] = relays.relay3;
  relay_obj["relay_4_humidity"] = relays.relay4;

  serializeJson(doc, Serial);
  Serial.println();
}

String getModeString(MissionMode mode) {
  switch (mode) {
    case PATROL:      return "PATROL";
    case SURVEILLANCE: return "SURVEILLANCE";
    case STEALTH:     return "STEALTH";
    case ALERT:       return "ALERT";
    default:          return "UNKNOWN";
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// COMMAND PROCESSING
// ============================================================================

void processIncomingCommands() {
  if (Serial.available()) {
    String command = Serial.readStringUntil('\n');
    command.trim();

    StaticJsonDocument<256> cmd_doc;
    DeserializationError error = deserializeJson(cmd_doc, command);

    if (error) {
      Serial.print("{\"error\":\"Parse failed: ");
      Serial.print(error.c_str());
      Serial.println("\"}");
      return;
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("set_mode")) {
      String mode_str = cmd_doc["set_mode"];
      if (mode_str == "PATROL")           system_state.currentMode = PATROL;
      else if (mode_str == "SURVEILLANCE") system_state.currentMode = SURVEILLANCE;
      else if (mode_str == "STEALTH")     system_state.currentMode = STEALTH;
      else if (mode_str == "ALERT")       system_state.currentMode = ALERT;
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Mode changed\"}");
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("set_baseline")) {
      baseline_temp = cmd_doc["baseline_temp"] | 20.0;
      baseline_humidity = cmd_doc["baseline_humidity"] | 50.0;
      baseline_set = true;
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Baseline reset\"}");
    }

    if (cmd_doc.containsKey("relay_override")) {
      int relay_num = cmd_doc["relay_num"];
      boolean state = cmd_doc["state"];

      switch (relay_num) {
        case 1: digitalWrite(RELAY_1_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay1 = state; break;
        case 2: digitalWrite(RELAY_2_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay2 = state; break;
        case 3: digitalWrite(RELAY_3_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay3 = state; break;
        case 4: digitalWrite(RELAY_4_PIN, state ? LOW : HIGH); relays.relay4 = state; break;
      }
      Serial.println("{\"status\":\"Relay updated\"}");
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

float getAverage(float* array, int size) {
  float sum = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    sum += array[i];
  }
  return sum / size;
}

/*
 * FIXES APPLIED:
 * 1. Relay init changed to LOW (active-low = ON at boot)
 * 2. baseline_set flag added — first real DHT read sets baseline correctly
 * 3. smoothing_index now increments after every read cycle
 * 4. DHT11 2-second minimum interval enforced via last_dht_read timer
 * 5. threat_stack "load" now uses powerConsumption (consistent with threat calc)
 *
 * NOTE: ESP32/LCD integration not included here.
 * Send telemetry to ESP32 via SoftwareSerial or I2C separately.
 *
 * SERIAL COMMAND FORMAT (Dashboard → Arduino):
 * {"set_mode": "STEALTH"}
 * {"relay_override": {"relay_num": 3, "state": false}}
 * {"set_baseline": {"baseline_temp": 20.5, "baseline_humidity": 48.0}}
 */

[22/05/26, 1:50:46 PM] Udit Jain : Create a professional, modern, and high-impact pitch deck presentation for an Ideathon. The presentation is for a product called "Sentinel Prime" (Repository Health Intelligence). 

Use a sleek, engineering-first, and professional tone. Structure the presentation into exactly 8 slides with the following detailed content and layout:

---
Slide 1: Title & Hook
- Title: Sentinel Prime
- Subtitle: Unified Engineering Intelligence & Repository Health Platform
- Visual Cue: A dark-themed, sleek abstract network graph representing codebases.
- Key Message: Moving from reactive firefighting to proactive, AI-driven codebase governance.

Slide 2: The Problem (The Hidden Cost of Tech Debt & Silos)
- Headline: The Codebase Blindspot
- Key Pain Points:
  1. Silent Architectural Decay: Circular imports and dependency sprawl slow down release cycles.
  2. The "Bus Factor" Risk: Critical knowledge silos where only 1-2 developers understand core systems.
  3. High-Churn Hotspots: Bug-prone files with high complexity and frequent changes that break constantly.
  4. Context Overhead: Technical managers lack real-time, actionable insights into repo health.

Slide 3: The Solution (Introducing Sentinel Prime)
- Headline: A Single Pane of Glass for Codebase Health
- Core Value: Sentinel Prime mines git commit history and parses dependency imports to compute real-time, actionable codebase health metrics.
- Key Pillars:
  - Repository Health Score (0-100 index based on complexity, churn, and architecture).
  - Codebase Hotspot Analysis.
  - Developer Collaboration Graph.
  - AI-Driven Architectural Copilot.

Slide 4: Core Features & Capabilities
- Headline: Features Built for Modern Engineering Teams
- Bullet Points:
  - Code & Architecture Health Index: Custom algorithms calculating complexity deltas and file churn.
  - Interactive Hotspots: Visualizing files with high complexity and frequent updates to prevent production regressions.
  - Developer Collaboration Graph: Network visualization mapping authors to files to detect knowledge silos.
  - Sentinel Copilot: A context-driven RAG AI assistant (using Vector DB and LLMs like DeepSeek & Groq) providing architectural explanations of repository health changes.

Slide 5: Technical Architecture
- Headline: Under the Hood of Sentinel Prime
- Architecture Flow:
  1. Git Mining Engine: Extracts and parses local/remote git commits and file modifications.
  2. AST Analyzer: Maps import/export dependencies to identify architectural circular loops.
  3. LLM API & Vector DB: Runs RAG queries on codebase metadata using high-performance open-source models (Groq/DeepSeek).
  4. Modern Web App Frontend: A glassmorphic, responsive, reactive dashboard built with React/Vite.

Slide 6: Target Market & User Personas
- Headline: Who Needs Sentinel Prime?
- Personas:
  - Engineering Managers & VPs: To track team health, bus factors, and maintain development velocity.
  - Tech Leads & Architects: To spot architectural drift, high-risk commits, and circular dependencies early.
  - Startup Founders: To keep codebase quality high during rapid scaling.

Slide 7: Business Model & ROI
- Headline: Maximizing Developer Velocity & ROI
- Value Proposition:
  - Reduced onboarding time by visualizing codebase structure.
  - Preventing expensive production outages by spotting "Most Dangerous Commits".
  - Proactive technical debt management instead of expensive rewrites.
- Delivery Model: SaaS-based developer tool (integration with GitHub Actions, GitLab CI/CD, and standalone web dashboards).

Slide 8: The Vision & Future Roadmap
- Headline: The Future of Autonomous Code Governance
- Timeline:
  - Phase 1: Real-time Repository Diagnostics & Sentinel Copilot (Current).
  - Phase 2: IDE Integrations (VS Code / JetBrains) warning developers of high-churn risks pre-commit.
  - Phase 3: Auto-refactoring agents that proactively fix architectural coupling.
- Closing Statement: "Sentinel Prime: Giving engineers the map to navigate their own code."

[20/06/26, 9:19:04 PM] Udit Jain : Forecasting and/or Nowcasting of Solar Flares using combined Soft and Hard X-ray data from Aditya-L1
Description
Solar flares are sudden, intense bursts of radiation originating from the release of magnetic energy in the solar atmosphere. These events can trigger severe space weather disruptions, impacting Earth-bound satellite communications, GPS navigation, and power grids. Anticipating these disruptions is critical for infrastructure safeguarding.
ISRO's Aditya-L1 mission continuously monitors the Sun from Lagrange Point L1 using two X-ray payloads: SoLEXS (Solar Low Energy X-ray Spectrometer, capturing soft X-rays) and HEL1OS (High Energy L1 Orbiting X-ray Spectrometer, capturing hard X-rays).
This challenge requires participants to build an automated algorithmic pipeline that uses the combined time-series data from SoLEXS and HEL1OS to detect (nowcast) or predict (forecast) these solar flares.
Objectives
To design an automated solar flare detection algorithm for the nowcasting (real-time detection and classification) of solar flares using soft and hard X-ray data.
To build a predictive algorithm for the forecasting of solar flares by identifying precursor patterns in soft and hard X-ray light curves before actual solar flare occurs.
Expected Outcomes
An automated database of nowcasted solar flares, generated by combining soft and hard X-ray light curves.
A trained model capable of forecasting flares with a quantifiable lead time.
Interface that visualizes the X-ray light curves and triggers with visual alerts when a flare is nowcasted or forecasted.
Dataset Required
Primary Data: Aditya-L1 SoLEXS (Level-1) and HEL1OS (Level-1) data, accessible via the ISRO ISSDC PRADAN portal.
Participants can use any other supplementary datasets.
Suggested Tools/Technologies
SoLEXS and HEL1OS data processing software accessible via the ISRO ISSDC PRADAN portal.
Any other open-source tools.
Expected Solution / Steps to be followed to achieve the objectives
Download SoLEXS and HEL1OS from the ISSDC portal. Write scripts to read SoLEXS and HEL1OS time-series light curve data.
Visually understand typical solar flare light curve observed in soft and hard X-rays.
Algorithmic Nowcasting: Build algorithms to detect flares independently in both soft and hard X-rays. Combine these independent catalogues to generate a master catalogue.
Predictive Modelling: Train a time-series model using combined dataset to predict the probability of a flare occurring in the next N minutes.
Evaluation Criteria
Detection of low- and high-class flares
High True Positive Rate and low False Alarm Rate for predictions.
Lead Time of predictions: How many minutes before the flare peak the model triggers an alert.

[15/08/26, 2:39:47 PM] Udit Jain : TMC-2 Fore + Nadir + Aft 5 m imagery
→ Stereo matching
→ High-resolution DEM
→ Super-resolution / terrain refinement
→ 1 m × 1 m elevation grid
→ Slope + roughness + crater/boulder/hazard detection
→ Hazard map

[15/08/26, 2:42:11 PM] Udit Jain : TMC stereo DEM → DEM super-resolution → 1 m DEM
[15/08/26, 2:46:44 PM] Udit Jain : * TMC Fore: ~5 m calibrated image for stereo reconstruction.
* TMC Nadir: ~5 m calibrated image; primary TMC imagery.
* TMC Aft: ~5 m calibrated image for stereo reconstruction.
* TMC Orthoimage: ~5 m orthorectified image for image super-resolution.
* TMC Stereo DEM/DTM: ~10 m elevation data for DEM super-resolution.
* TMC Metadata: Camera geometry, Sun azimuth/elevation, timestamp, footprint and coordinates. Sih.docx
* OHRC Image: ~25–30 cm high-resolution image for SR supervision/validation. Sih.docx
* OHRC DEM: Sub-metre/reference DEM where available for DEM-SR validation. Sih.docx
* TMC–OHRC Pairs: Geographically aligned TMC + OHRC patches for training/validation. Sih.docx
* Crater Dataset: Lunar crater catalogue/images for detector pretraining. Sih.docx
* Boulder Dataset: Published lunar boulder data/images for detector pretraining. Sih.docx
[15/08/26, 2:48:02 PM] Udit Jain : 1. TMC Fore image — ~5 m
2. TMC Nadir image — ~5 m
3. TMC Aft image — ~5 m
4. TMC Stereo DEM/DTM — ~10 m
5. TMC Orthoimage — ~5 m
6. TMC metadata/geometry — Sun + camera information
[15/08/26, 2:50:17 PM] Udit Jain : OHRC datasets — only overlapping TMC regions
7. OHRC image — ~25–30 cm
8. OHRC-derived/reference DEM — sub-metre, where available
9. OHRC metadata — geometry and acquisition information Sih.docx

Auxiliary ML datasets
10. Lunar crater catalogue/images — for crater-detector pretraining
11. Lunar boulder catalogue/images — for boulder-detector pretraining
12. Optional Kaggle/academic lunar crater datasets — additional training diversity

Training Summary & Loss Convergence
Stage A (Reconstruction & Topographic Slope Pretraining)
Optimizer: Adam ($lr = 2 \times 10^{-4}$), Cosine Annealing, trained on GPU (mps)

checkpoints/dem_sr_stage_a.pth
 & 

checkpoints/image_sr_stage_a.pth
Stage B (Adversarial & Photoclinometric Shading Consistency)
